HEADER FILE format-pad-native.h:
#ifndef BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#define BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ABI: r0..14=D0-D7/A0-A6,r15=original entrySP,r16=low16SR.
 * Implement void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r).
 * Assembly only captures/restores this frame. Body reproduces original
 * public D2 save at entrySP-4 and restore from that location, plus all
 * semantic register/CCR outputs. Arguments use their ORIGINAL widths/offsets:
 * destination LONG entrySP+4,character WORD entrySP+8,remaining pointer
 * LONG entrySP+10,width WORD entrySP+14. All target accesses fresh/ordered.
 * Full frame private; no host pointers/callbacks. No guards or loop bounds.
 */
#define PR8(a) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR16(a) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR32(a) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PW8(a,v) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint8_t)(v))
#define PW16(a,v) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint16_t)(v))
#define PW32(a,v) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint32_t)(v))
#endif

HEADER FILE m68k-fact-helpers.h:
#ifndef BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#define BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ISA primitives only, not a game-function implementation.
 * Valid widths for mf_logic:8,16,32. Word writes keep the incoming upper16.
 * Helpers affect the semantic frame only; target memory stays explicit.
 */
static inline void mf_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 r[index]=(r[index]&0xffff0000u)|value;
}
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
#endif

#include "format-pad-native.h"
#include "m68k-fact-helpers.h"

void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r)
{
    uint32_t entry_sp = r[15];

    /* 110944: move.l d2, -(a7) */
    PW32(entry_sp - 4, r[2]);

    /* 110946: movea.l $8(a7), a0 */
    r[8] = PR32(entry_sp + 4);

    /* 110950: movea.l $e(a7), a1 */
    r[9] = PR32(entry_sp + 10);

    /* 110954: move.w $12(a7), d1 */
    mf_word(r, 1, PR16(entry_sp + 14));

    /* 110958: moveq #$1, d2 */
    r[2] = 1u;
    mf_logic(r, 1u, 32);

    /* 110960: tst.w (a1) */
    mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16);

    /* 110962: bgt.b $1b178 */
    if ((r[16] & 0x01) == 0 && ((r[16] & 0x08) == (r[16] & 0x02)))
        goto label_110968;
    else
        goto label_110964;

label_110964:
    /* 110964: clr.w d0 */
    mf_word(r, 0, 0);
    mf_logic(r, 0u, 16);

    /* 110966: bra.b $1b19c */
    goto label_111004;

label_110968:
    /* 110968: move.b $d(a7), (a0)+ */
    PW8(r[8], PR8(entry_sp + 9));
    r[8] += 1u;

    /* 110972: move.w (a1), d0 */
    mf_word(r, 0, PR16(r[9]));
    mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16);

    /* 110974: subq.w #$1, d0 */
    mf_sub16(r, (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu), 1u);

    /* 110976: move.w d0, (a1) */
    PW16(r[9], (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu));
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu), 16);

    /* 110978: ble.b $1b19a */
    if ((r[16] & 0x01) != 0 || ((r[16] & 0x08) != (r[16] & 0x02)))
        goto label_111002;
    else
        goto label_110980;

label_110980:
    /* 110980: subq.w #$1, d1 */
    mf_sub16(r, (uint16_t)(r[1] & 0xffffu), 1u);

    /* 110982: tst.w d1 */
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)(r[1] & 0xffffu), 16);

    /* 110984: ble.b $1b19a */
    if ((r[16] & 0x01) != 0 || ((r[16] & 0x08) != (r[16] & 0x02)))
        goto label_111002;
    else
        goto label_110986;

label_110986:
    /* 110986: move.b #$20, (a0)+ */
    PW8(r[8], 32u);
    r[8] += 1u;

    /* 110990: move.w (a1), d0 */
    mf_word(r, 0, PR16(r[9]));
    mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16);

    /* 110992: subq.w #$1, d0 */
    mf_sub16(r, (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu), 1u);

    /* 110994: move.w d0, (a1) */
    PW16(r[9], (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu));
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu), 16);

    /* 110996: addq.w #$1, d2 */
    mf_add16(r, (uint16_t)(r[2] & 0xffffu), 1u);

    /* 110998: tst.w d0 */
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)(r[0] & 0xffffu), 16);

    /* 111000: bgt.b $1b184 */
    if ((r[16] & 0x01) == 0 && ((r[16] & 0x08) == (r[16] & 0x02)))
        goto label_110980;
    else
        goto label_111002;

label_111002:
    /* 111002: move.w d2, d0 */
    mf_word(r, 0, (uint16_t)(r[2] & 0xffffu));
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)(r[2] & 0xffffu), 16);

label_111004:
    /* 111004: move.l (a7)+, d2 */
    r[2] = PR32(entry_sp - 4);

    /* 111006: rts */

 * public D2 save at entrySP-4 and restore from that location, plus all
 * semantic register/CCR outputs. Arguments use their ORIGINAL widths/offsets:
 * destination LONG entrySP+4,character WORD entrySP+8,remaining pointer
 * LONG entrySP+10,width WORD entrySP+14. All target accesses fresh/ordered.
 * Full frame private; no host pointers/callbacks. No guards or loop bounds.
 */
#define PR8(a) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR16(a) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR32(a) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PW8(a,v) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint8_t)(v))
#define PW16(a,v) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint16_t)(v))
#define PW32(a,v) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint32_t)(v))
#endif

HEADER FILE m68k-fact-helpers.h:
#ifndef BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#define BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ISA primitives only, not a game-function implementation.
 * Valid widths for mf_logic:8,16,32. Word writes keep the incoming upper16.
 * Helpers affect the semantic frame only; target memory stays explicit.
 */
static inline void mf_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 r[index]=(r[index]&0xffff0000u)|value;
}
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
#endif

Repair the complete file. Do not weaken checks, invent test success or replace original behavior with a guard.
RESPONSE
The error `AssertionError('Format padding did not return')` indicates that the function `biwn_native_format_pad_body` did not complete execution or returned an invalid state.

Looking at the previous code and the tool facts:
1.  **Instruction `110958: moveq #$1, d2`**: The previous code used `mf_logic(r, 1u, 32)`. The `mf_logic` helper sets CCR bits based on the value. For `moveq`, the CCR is updated as if a logical operation occurred. However, `moveq` is a 32-bit register write. The helper `mf_logic` takes a `value` and `bits`. If we pass `1u` and `32`, it sets N=0, Z=0, V=0, C=0. This is correct for `moveq #1`.
2.  **Instruction `110960: tst.w (a1)`**: The previous code used `mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16)`. This is correct.
3.  **Branch `110962: bgt.b`**: The condition `gt` is `Z==0 && N==V`. The previous code checked `(r[16] & 0x01) == 0` (Z==0) and `(r[16] & 0x08) == (r[16] & 0x02)` (N==V). This is correct.
4.  **Instruction `110964: clr.w d0`**: The previous code used `mf_word(r, 0, 0)` and `mf_logic(r, 0u, 16)`. `clr` sets the register to 0 and updates CCR as if `AND #0`. `mf_logic` with 0 sets Z=1, N=0, V=0, C=0. This is correct.
5.  **Instruction `110966: bra.b`**: Unconditional branch.
6.  **Instruction `110968: move.b $d(a7), (a0)+`**: Writes byte to memory, increments A0.
7.  **Instruction `110972: move.w (a1), d0`**: Reads word from A1, writes to D0. Updates CCR.
8.  **Instruction `110974: subq.w #$1, d0`**: Subtracts 1 from D0. Updates CCR.
9.  **Instruction `110976: move.w d0, (a1)`**: Writes D0 to A1. Updates CCR.
10. **Branch `110978: ble.b`**: Condition `le` is `Z==1 || N!=V`.
11. **Instruction `110980: subq.w #$1, d1`**: Subtracts 1 from D1.
12. **Instruction `110982: tst.w d1`**: Tests D1.
13. **Branch `110984: ble.b`**: Condition `le`.
14. **Instruction `110986: move.b #$20, (a0)+`**: Writes 0x20 to A0, increments A0.
15. **Instruction `110990: move.w (a1), d0`**: Reads word from A1.
16. **Instruction `110992: subq.w #$1, d0`**: Subtracts 1 from D0.
17. **Instruction `110994: move.w d0, (a1)`**: Writes D0 to A1.
18. **Instruction `110996: addq.w #$1, d2`**: Adds 1 to D2.
19. **Instruction `110998: tst.w d0`**: Tests D0.
20. **Branch `111000: bgt.b`**: Condition `gt`.
21. **Instruction `111002: move.w d2, d0`**: Moves D2 to D0.
22. **Instruction `111004: move.l (a7)+, d2`**: Restores D2 from stack.
23. **Instruction `111006: rts`**: Returns.

The issue might be related to how the CCR is handled or the specific values. Let's re-examine `moveq`.
`moveq #$1, d2` sets D2 to 1. The CCR is updated.
`mf_logic(r, 1u, 32)` sets:
- `value = 1`
- `mask = 0xffffffff`
- `r[16] = (r[16] & ~15) | (1==0 ? 4 : 0) | ((1 & 0x80000000) ? 8 : 0)`
- `r[16] = (r[16] & ~15) | 0 | 0`
- So Z=0, N=0, V=0, C=0. This is correct.

Let's look at `tst.w (a1)`.
`mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16)`.
If `PR16(r[9])` is 0, Z=1, N=0, V=0, C=0.
If `PR16(r[9])` is > 0, Z=0, N=0, V=0, C=0.
If `PR16(r[9])` is < 0 (bit 15 set), Z=0, N=1, V=0, C=0.

The branch `bgt` checks `Z==0 && N==V`.
If `tst` resulted in Z=1 (value 0), `bgt` is false.
If `tst` resulted in Z=0 and N=0 (value > 0), `bgt` is true.
If `tst` resulted in Z=0 and N=1 (value < 0), `bgt` is false.

This logic seems correct.

However, there is a subtle point in the `mf_logic` helper provided in the header:
```c
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
```
It clears bits 0-3 (C, V, Z, N) and sets them based on `value`. It preserves X (bit 4) and other bits. This matches the "logic" CCR rule: N=result sign, Z=zero, V=C=0, X preserved.

Let's check `subq.w`.
`mf_sub16` is used.
```c
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
```
It clears bits 0-4 (C, V, Z, N, X) and sets them.
Wait, `sub` rule says: "width-wrapped subtraction;C/X borrow;V signed overflow;N/Z result".
The helper `mf_sub16` sets:
- `a<b ? 17u : 0u` -> If borrow, sets C (bit 0) and X (bit 4). `17 = 10001b`. So C=1, X=1. If no borrow, C=0, X=0.
- `((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u` -> V (bit 1).
- `value==0u?4u:0u` -> Z (bit 2).
- `(value&0x8000u)?8u:0u` -> N (bit 3).
This matches the `sub` CCR rule.

Let's check `addq.w`.
`mf_add16` is used.
```c
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
```
Similar to sub, but for add.
- `sum>65535u?17u:0u` -> If carry, sets C and X.
- `((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u` -> V.
- Z, N.
This matches the `add` CCR rule.

The previous code seems logically sound based on the helpers. Why did it fail?
"Format padding did not return" might imply an infinite loop or a crash.

1. `moveq #1, d2` -> D2=1.
2. `tst.w (a1)` -> Check if remaining count is > 0.
3. If `bgt` (count > 0):

   - `move.w (a1), d0` -> Load count.
   - `subq.w #1, d0` -> Decrement count.
   - `move.w d0, (a1)` -> Store count.
   - `ble.b` -> If count <= 0, exit loop.

   - `ble.b` -> If width <= 0, exit loop.

   - `move.w (a1), d0` -> Load count.

   - `clr.w d0` -> D0=0.
   - `bra.b` -> Jump to `111004`.
5. `111002: move.w d2, d0` -> D0 = D2.
6. `111004: move.l (a7)+, d2` -> Restore D2.
7. `111006: rts`.

The loop structure:
- Outer check: `tst.w (a1)` (count). If count > 0, enter.
- Inside:
  - Copy char.
  - Decrement count.
  - If count <= 0, exit.
  - Decrement width (D1).
  - If width <= 0, exit.
  - Write space.
  - Decrement count.
  - Increment D2.
  - If count > 0, loop back to `110980` (which is `subq.w #1, d1`).

1. `subq.w #1, d1`
2. `tst.w d1`
3. `ble.b` exit
4. `move.b #$20, (a0)+`
5. `move.w (a1), d0`
6. `subq.w #1, d0`
7. `move.w d0, (a1)`
8. `addq.w #1, d2`
9. `tst.w d0`
10. `bgt.b` back to 1.

This looks like a valid loop. It decrements D1 and D0 (count) each iteration.
If D1 or D0 becomes <= 0, it exits.
Since D1 and D0 are 16-bit, they will eventually underflow and become large positive numbers if they wrap around, but `ble` checks for <= 0.
If D1 is 1, `subq` makes it 0. `tst` sets Z=1. `ble` is true (Z=1). Exit.
If D1 is 0, `subq` makes it 0xFFFF. `tst` sets N=1, Z=0. `ble` is true (N!=V, V=0). Exit.
So the loop terminates.

static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){

Let's re-examine `moveq`.\n`moveq #$1, d2` sets D2 to 1. The CCR is updated.\n`mf_logic(r, 1u, 32)` sets:\n- `value = 1`\n- `mask = 0xffffffff`\n- `r[16] = (r[16] & ~15) | (1==0 ? 4 : 0) | ((1 & 0x80000000) ? 8 : 0)`\n- `r[16] = (r[16] & ~15) | 0 | 0`\n- So Z=0, N=0, V=0, C=0. This is correct.\n\nLet's look at `tst.w (a1)`.\n`mf_logic(r, PR16(r[9]), 16)`.\nIf `PR16(r[9])` is 0, Z=1, N=0, V=0, C=0.\nIf `PR16(r[9])` is > 0, Z=0, N=0, V=0, C=0.\nIf `PR16(r[9])` is < 0 (bit 15 set), Z=0, N=1, V=0, C=0.\n\nThe branch `bgt` checks `Z==0 && N==V`.\nIf `tst` resulted in Z=1 (value 0), `bgt` is false.\nIf `tst` resulted in Z=0 and N=0 (value > 0), `bgt` is true.\nIf `tst` resulted in Z=0 and N=1 (value < 0), `bgt` is false.\n\nThis logic seems correct.\n\nHowever, there is a subtle point in the `mf_logic` helper provided in the header:\n```c\nstatic inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){\n uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);\n value&=mask;\n r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);\n}\n```\nIt clears bits 0-3 (C, V, Z, N) and sets them based on `value`. It preserves X (bit 4) and other bits. This matches the \"logic\" CCR rule: N=result sign, Z=zero, V=C=0, X preserved.\n\nLet's check `subq.w`.\n`mf_sub16` is used.\n```c\nstatic inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){\n uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);\n r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|\n  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);\n return value;\n}\n```\nIt clears bits 0-4 (C, V, Z, N, X) and sets them.\nWait, `sub` rule says: \"width-wrapped subtraction;C/X borrow;V signed overflow;N/Z result\".\nThe helper `mf_sub16` sets:\n- `a<b ? 17u : 0u` -> If borrow, sets C (bit 0) and X (bit 4). `17 = 10001b`. So C=1, X=1. HEADER FILE format-pad-native.h:
#ifndef BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#define BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ABI: r0..14=D0-D7/A0-A6,r15=original entrySP,r16=low16SR.
 * Implement void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r).
 * Assembly only captures/restores this frame. Body reproduces original
 * public D2 save at entrySP-4 and restore from that location, plus all
 * semantic register/CCR outputs. Arguments use their ORIGINAL widths/offsets:
 * destination LONG entrySP+4,character WORD entrySP+8,remaining pointer
 * LONG entrySP+10,width WORD entrySP+14. All target accesses fresh/ordered.
 * Full frame private; no host pointers/callbacks. No guards or loop bounds.
 */
#define PR8(a) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR16(a) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR32(a) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PW8(a,v) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint8_t)(v))
#define PW16(a,v) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint16_t)(v))
#define PW32(a,v) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint32_t)(v))
#endif

HEADER FILE m68k-fact-helpers.h:
#ifndef BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#define BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ISA primitives only, not a game-function implementation.
 * Valid widths for mf_logic:8,16,32. Word writes keep the incoming upper16.
 * mf_sub16/mf_add16 RETURN a word and update CCR ONLY; they do not store
 * any data register. Prefer mf_sub_word/mf_add_word below for Dn updates.
 * Helpers affect the semantic frame only; target memory stays explicit.
 */
static inline void mf_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 r[index]=(r[index]&0xffff0000u)|value;
}
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
/* Whole register/CCR effects for the named ISA operation; source values
 * passed to these functions are evaluated once, including volatile reads.
 * MOVEA is intentionally absent: assign An without changing CCR.
 */
static inline void mf_move16(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 mf_word(r,index,value);mf_logic(r,value,16);
}
static inline void mf_move32(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint32_t value){
 r[index]=value;mf_logic(r,value,32);
}
static inline void mf_sub_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_sub16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline void mf_add_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_add16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline uint32_t mf_gt(const uint32_t *r){
 return !(r[16]&4u)&&(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)==((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}

 return (r[16]&4u)||(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)!=((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}

ISA API usage:MOVE.W to Dn can use mf_move16(r,index,value),which writes the low word AND CCR. MOVE.L/MOVEQ to Dn can use mf_move32. MOVEA is plain assignment with no CCR change. SUBQ.W/ADDQ.W to Dn can use mf_sub_word/mf_add_word,which store the result and CCR. Lower-level mf_sub16/mf_add16 return a word;discarding that return loses the data-register update. Use mf_gt/mf_le for normalized signed CCR predicates. Evaluate each volatile read exactly once for its original instruction;do not read it again just to calculate flags. Keep the C concise;per-instruction comments are optional.

HEADER FILE format-pad-native.h:
#ifndef BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#define BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ABI: r0..14=D0-D7/A0-A6,r15=original entrySP,r16=low16SR.
 * Implement void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r).
 * Assembly only captures/restores this frame. Body reproduces original
 * public D2 save at entrySP-4 and restore from that location, plus all
 * semantic register/CCR outputs. Arguments use their ORIGINAL widths/offsets:
 * destination LONG entrySP+4,character WORD entrySP+8,remaining pointer
 * LONG entrySP+10,width WORD entrySP+14. All target accesses fresh/ordered.
 * Full frame private; no host pointers/callbacks. No guards or loop bounds.
 */
#define PR8(a) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR16(a) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR32(a) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PW8(a,v) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint8_t)(v))
#define PW16(a,v) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint16_t)(v))
#define PW32(a,v) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint32_t)(v))
#endif

HEADER FILE m68k-fact-helpers.h:
#ifndef BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#define BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ISA primitives only, not a game-function implementation.
 * Valid widths for mf_logic:8,16,32. Word writes keep the incoming upper16.
 * mf_sub16/mf_add16 RETURN a word and update CCR ONLY; they do not store
 * any data register. Prefer mf_sub_word/mf_add_word below for Dn updates.
 * Helpers affect the semantic frame only; target memory stays explicit.
 */
static inline void mf_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 r[index]=(r[index]&0xffff0000u)|value;
}
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
/* Whole register/CCR effects for the named ISA operation; source values
 * passed to these functions are evaluated once, including volatile reads.
 * MOVEA is intentionally absent: assign An without changing CCR.
 */
static inline void mf_move16(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 mf_word(r,index,value);mf_logic(r,value,16);
}
static inline void mf_move32(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint32_t value){
 r[index]=value;mf_logic(r,value,32);
}
static inline void mf_sub_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_sub16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline void mf_add_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_add16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline uint32_t mf_gt(const uint32_t *r){
 return !(r[16]&4u)&&(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)==((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}
static inline uint32_t mf_le(const uint32_t *r){
 return (r[16]&4u)||(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)!=((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}

ISA API usage:MOVE.W to Dn can use mf_move16(r,index,value),which writes the low word AND CCR. MOVE.L/MOVEQ to Dn can use mf_move32. MOVEA is plain assignment with no CCR change. SUBQ.W/ADDQ.W to Dn can use mf_sub_word/mf_add_word,which store the result and CCR. Lower-level mf_sub16/mf_add16 return a word;discarding that return loses the data-register update. Use mf_gt/mf_le for normalized signed CCR predicates. Evaluate each volatile read exactly once for its original instruction;do not read it again just to calculate flags. Keep the C concise;per-instruction comments are optional.

#include "format-pad-native.h"
#include "m68k-fact-helpers.h"

void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r) {
    uint32_t entry_sp = r[15];
    uint32_t a0, a1;

    PW32(entry_sp - 4, r[2]);
    a0 = PR32(entry_sp + 4);
    a1 = PR32(entry_sp + 10);
    mf_move16(r, 1, PR16(entry_sp + 14));
    mf_move32(r, 2, 1u);

    mf_logic(r, PR16(a1), 16);
    if (mf_gt(r)) {
        PW8(a0, PR8(entry_sp + 9));
        a0 += 1u;
        mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
        mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
        PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
        if (!mf_le(r)) {
            mf_sub_word(r, 1, 1u);
            mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[1], 16);
            if (!mf_le(r)) {
                PW8(a0, 32u);
                a0 += 1u;
                mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
                mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
                PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
                mf_add_word(r, 2, 1u);
                mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[0], 16);
                if (mf_gt(r)) {
                    goto loop_start;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        mf_word(r, 0, 0u);
        mf_logic(r, 0u, 16);
    }

    mf_move16(r, 0, (uint16_t)r[2]);
    r[2] = PR32(entry_sp - 4);
    mf_logic(r, r[2], 32);
    return;

loop_start:
    mf_sub_word(r, 1, 1u);
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[1], 16);
    if (!mf_le(r)) {
        PW8(a0, 32u);
        a0 += 1u;
        mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
        mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
        PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
        mf_add_word(r, 2, 1u);
        mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[0], 16);
        if (mf_gt(r)) {
            goto loop_start;
        }
    }
    mf_move16(r, 0, (uint16_t)r[2]);
    r[2] = PR32(entry_sp - 4);
    mf_logic(r, r[2], 32);
    return;
}

HEADER FILE format-pad-native.h:
#ifndef BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#define BIWN_FORMAT_PAD_NATIVE_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ABI: r0..14=D0-D7/A0-A6,r15=original entrySP,r16=low16SR.
 * Implement void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r).
 * Assembly only captures/restores this frame. Body reproduces original
 * public D2 save at entrySP-4 and restore from that location, plus all
 * semantic register/CCR outputs. Arguments use their ORIGINAL widths/offsets:
 * destination LONG entrySP+4,character WORD entrySP+8,remaining pointer
 * LONG entrySP+10,width WORD entrySP+14. All target accesses fresh/ordered.
 * Full frame private; no host pointers/callbacks. No guards or loop bounds.
 */
#define PR8(a) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR16(a) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PR32(a) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a))
#define PW8(a,v) (*(volatile uint8_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint8_t)(v))
#define PW16(a,v) (*(volatile uint16_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint16_t)(v))
#define PW32(a,v) (*(volatile uint32_t *)(uint32_t)(a)=(uint32_t)(v))
#endif

HEADER FILE m68k-fact-helpers.h:
#ifndef BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#define BIWN_M68K_FACT_HELPERS_H
#include <stdint.h>
/* Coordinator ISA primitives only, not a game-function implementation.
 * Valid widths for mf_logic:8,16,32. Word writes keep the incoming upper16.
 * mf_sub16/mf_add16 RETURN a word and update CCR ONLY; they do not store
 * any data register. Prefer mf_sub_word/mf_add_word below for Dn updates.
 * Helpers affect the semantic frame only; target memory stays explicit.
 */
static inline void mf_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 r[index]=(r[index]&0xffff0000u)|value;
}
static inline void mf_logic(uint32_t *r,uint32_t value,unsigned bits){
 uint32_t mask=bits==32?0xffffffffu:((1u<<bits)-1u);
 value&=mask;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~15u)|(value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&(1u<<(bits-1u)))?8u:0u);
}
static inline uint16_t mf_sub16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint16_t value=(uint16_t)((uint32_t)a-b);
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(a<b?17u:0u)|(((a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
static inline uint16_t mf_add16(uint32_t *r,uint16_t a,uint16_t b){
 uint32_t sum=(uint32_t)a+b;uint16_t value=(uint16_t)sum;
 r[16]=(r[16]&~31u)|(sum>65535u?17u:0u)|((~(a^b)&(a^value)&0x8000u)?2u:0u)|
  (value==0u?4u:0u)|((value&0x8000u)?8u:0u);
 return value;
}
/* Whole register/CCR effects for the named ISA operation; source values
 * passed to these functions are evaluated once, including volatile reads.
 * MOVEA is intentionally absent: assign An without changing CCR.
 */
static inline void mf_move16(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 mf_word(r,index,value);mf_logic(r,value,16);
}
static inline void mf_move32(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint32_t value){
 r[index]=value;mf_logic(r,value,32);
}
static inline void mf_sub_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_sub16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline void mf_add_word(uint32_t *r,unsigned index,uint16_t value){
 uint16_t result=mf_add16(r,(uint16_t)r[index],value);mf_word(r,index,result);
}
static inline uint32_t mf_gt(const uint32_t *r){
 return !(r[16]&4u)&&(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)==((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}
static inline uint32_t mf_le(const uint32_t *r){
 return (r[16]&4u)||(((r[16]&8u)!=0u)!=((r[16]&2u)!=0u));
}
#endif

ISA API usage:MOVE.W to Dn can use mf_move16(r,index,value),which writes the low word AND CCR. MOVE.L/MOVEQ to Dn can use mf_move32. MOVEA is plain assignment with no CCR change. SUBQ.W/ADDQ.W to Dn can use mf_sub_word/mf_add_word,which store the result and CCR. Lower-level mf_sub16/mf_add16 return a word;discarding that return loses the data-register update. Use mf_gt/mf_le for normalized signed CCR predicates. Evaluate each volatile read exactly once for its original instruction;do not read it again just to calculate flags. Keep the C concise;per-instruction comments are optional.
Previous complete C:
#include "format-pad-native.h"
#include "m68k-fact-helpers.h"

void biwn_native_format_pad_body(uint32_t *r) {
    uint32_t entry_sp = r[15];
    uint32_t a0, a1;

    PW32(entry_sp - 4, r[2]);
    a0 = PR32(entry_sp + 4);
    a1 = PR32(entry_sp + 10);
    mf_move16(r, 1, PR16(entry_sp + 14));
    mf_move32(r, 2, 1u);

    mf_logic(r, PR16(a1), 16);
    if (mf_gt(r)) {
        PW8(a0, PR8(entry_sp + 9));
        a0 += 1u;
        mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
        mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
        PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
        if (!mf_le(r)) {
            mf_sub_word(r, 1, 1u);
            mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[1], 16);
            if (!mf_le(r)) {
                PW8(a0, 32u);
                a0 += 1u;
                mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
                mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
                PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
                mf_add_word(r, 2, 1u);
                mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[0], 16);
                if (mf_gt(r)) {
                    goto loop_start;
                }
            }
        }
    } else {
        mf_word(r, 0, 0u);
        mf_logic(r, 0u, 16);
    }

    mf_move16(r, 0, (uint16_t)r[2]);
    r[2] = PR32(entry_sp - 4);
    mf_logic(r, r[2], 32);
    return;

loop_start:
    mf_sub_word(r, 1, 1u);
    mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[1], 16);
    if (!mf_le(r)) {
        PW8(a0, 32u);
        a0 += 1u;
        mf_move16(r, 0, PR16(a1));
        mf_sub_word(r, 0, 1u);
        PW16(a1, (uint16_t)r[0]);
        mf_add_word(r, 2, 1u);
        mf_logic(r, (uint16_t)r[0], 16);
        if (mf_gt(r)) {
            goto loop_start;
        }
    }
    mf_move16(r, 0, (uint16_t)r[2]);
    r[2] = PR32(entry_sp - 4);
    mf_logic(r, r[2], 32);
    return;
}
